CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 MUJOCO_GL=egl python eval_oft_prune_libero.py \\\\\\n        --k_ratio 0.5 --suite libero_object --num_episodes 50 --seed 7\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse, json, math, os, sys, time as time_mod\\nfrom collections import deque\\n\\nos.environ.setdefault(\\\"MUJOCO_GL\\\", \\\"egl\\\")\\nos.environ.setdefault(\\\"TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE\\\", \\\"1\\\")\\nos.environ.setdefault(\\\"HF_HUB_OFFLINE\\\", \\\"1\\\")\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\nfrom PIL import Image\\n\\nDEVICE = torch.device(\\\"cuda:0\\\")\\nCHECKPOINT_PATH = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/openvla-oft-libero-object\\\"\\nUNNORM_KEY = \\\"libero_object\\\"\\n\\n# OFT LIBERO constants\\nACTION_DIM = 7\\nPROPRIO_DIM = 8\\nNUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK = 8\\nNUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS = 8\\nNUM_IMAGES_IN_INPUT = 2\\nUSE_PROPRIO = True\\n\\nSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\\n    \\\"libero_spatial\\\": 220,\\n    \\\"libero_object\\\": 280,\\n    \\\"libero_goal\\\": 300,\\n    \\\"libero_10\\\": 520,\\n}\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Action head / projector classes (from openvla-oft, inlined to avoid tf dep)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nclass MLPResNetBlock(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, dim):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.ffn = nn.Sequential(nn.LayerNorm(dim), nn.Linear(dim, dim), nn.ReLU())\\n\\n    def forward(self, x):\\n        return x + self.ffn(x)\\n\\n\\nclass MLPResNet(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, num_blocks, input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.layer_norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(input_dim)\\n        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim)\\n        self.relu = nn.ReLU()\\n        self.mlp_resnet_blocks = nn.ModuleList([MLPResNetBlock(dim=hidden_dim) for _ in range(num_blocks)])\\n        self.layer_norm2 = nn.LayerNorm(hidden_dim)\\n        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, output_dim)\\n\\n    def forward(self, x):\\n        x = self.relu(self.fc1(self.layer_norm1(x)))\\n        for block in self.mlp_resnet_blocks:\\n            x = block(x)\\n        return self.fc2(self.layer_norm2(x))\\n\\n\\nclass L1RegressionActionHead(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, input_dim=4096, hidden_dim=4096, action_dim=7):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.action_dim = action_dim\\n        self.model = MLPResNet(\\n            num_blocks=2, input_dim=input_dim * ACTION_DIM, hidden_dim=hidden_dim, output_dim=action_dim\\n        )\\n\\n    def predict_action(self, actions_hidden_states):\\n        batch_size = actions_hidden_states.shape[0]\\n        rearranged = actions_hidden_states.reshape(batch_size, NUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK, -1)\\n        return self.model(rearranged)\\n\\n\\nclass ProprioProjector(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, llm_dim, proprio_dim):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(proprio_dim, llm_dim, bias=True)\\n        self.act_fn1 = nn.GELU()\\n        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(llm_dim, llm_dim, bias=True)\\n\\n    def forward(self, proprio):\\n        return self.fc2(self.act_fn1(self.fc1(proprio)))\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Pruning hook\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nclass RandomPruningHook:\\n    def __init__(self, k_ratio=0.5):\\n        self.k_ratio = k_ratio\\n        self.logged = False\\n\\n    def __call__(self, module, input, output):\\n        B, N, D = output.shape\\n        keep = max(1, int(N * self.k_ratio))\\n        indices = torch.randperm(N, device=output.device)[:keep].sort().values\\n        pruned = output[:, indices, :]\\n        if not self.logged:\\n            print(f\\\"[Pruning] {N} -> {keep} vision tokens (k={self.k_ratio})\\\", flush=True)\\n            self.logged = True\\n        return pruned\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Args / seed / model loading\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef parse_args():\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--k_ratio\\\", type=float, required=True)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--suite\\\", default=\\\"libero_object\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--num_episodes\\\", type=int, default=50)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=7)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--output\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--num_steps_wait\\\", type=int, default=10)\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\ndef set_seed(seed):\\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\\n    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\\n    np.random.seed(seed)\\n    import random\\n    random.seed(seed)\\n    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\\n    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\\n\\n\\ndef load_model():\\n    from transformers import AutoModelForVision2Seq, AutoProcessor\\n\\n    print(f\\\"[*] Loading OFT model from: {CHECKPOINT_PATH}\\\", flush=True)\\n    vla = AutoModelForVision2Seq.from_pretrained(\\n        CHECKPOINT_PATH,\\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\\n    ).to(DEVICE)\\n    vla.eval()\\n\\n    if hasattr(vla, \\\"vision_backbone\\\") and hasattr(vla.vision_backbone, \\\"set_num_images_in_input\\\"):\\n        vla.vision_backbone.set_num_images_in_input(NUM_IMAGES_IN_INPUT)\\n        print(f\\\"[*] Set num_images_in_input={NUM_IMAGES_IN_INPUT}\\\", flush=True)\\n\\n    with open(os.path.join(CHECKPOINT_PATH, \\\"dataset_statistics.json\\\")) as f:\\n        vla.norm_stats = json.load(f)\\n\\n    processor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH, trust_remote_code=True)\\n\\n    llm_dim = vla.config.text_config.hidden_size\\n    print(f\\\"[*] LLM hidden dim: {llm_dim}\\\", flush=True)\\n\\n    # Action head\\n    action_head = L1RegressionActionHead(input_dim=llm_dim, hidden_dim=llm_dim, action_dim=ACTION_DIM)\\n    action_head = action_head.to(torch.bfloat16).to(DEVICE)\\n    action_head.eval()\\n    ah_path = os.path.join(CHECKPOINT_PATH, \\\"action_head--150000_checkpoint.pt\\\")\\n    ah_state = torch.load(ah_path, map_location=\\\"cpu\\\", weights_only=False)\\n    if \\\"state_dict\\\" in ah_state:\\n        ah_state = ah_state[\\\"state_dict\\\"]\\n    action_head.load_state_dict(ah_state)\\n    print(f\\\"[*] Action head loaded from: {ah_path}\\\", flush=True)\\n\\n    # Proprio projector\\n    proprio_proj = ProprioProjector(llm_dim=llm_dim, proprio_dim=PROPRIO_DIM)\\n    proprio_proj = proprio_proj.to(torch.bfloat16).to(DEVICE)\\n    proprio_proj.eval()\\n    pp_path = os.path.join(CHECKPOINT_PATH, \\\"proprio_projector--150000_checkpoint.pt\\\")\\n    pp_state = torch.load(pp_path, map_location=\\\"cpu\\\", weights_only=False)\\n    if \\\"state_dict\\\" in pp_state:\\n        pp_state = pp_state[\\\"state_dict\\\"]\\n    proprio_proj.load_state_dict(pp_state)\\n    print(f\\\"[*] Proprio projector loaded from: {pp_path}\\\", flush=True)\\n\\n    print(f\\\"[*] GPU mem: {torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1e9:.1f} GB\\\", flush=True)\\n    return vla, processor, action_head, proprio_proj\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Observation processing\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef center_crop_image(image, crop_scale=0.9):\\n    w, h = image.size\\n    new_w = int(w * math.sqrt(crop_scale))\\n    new_h = int(h * math.sqrt(crop_scale))\\n    left = (w - new_w) // 2\\n    top = (h - new_h) // 2\\n    return image.crop((left, top, left + new_w, top + new_h)).resize((w, h), Image.LANCZOS)\\n\\n\\ndef preprocess_image(obs, key=\\\"agentview_image\\\"):\\n    img = obs[key][::-1, ::-1]\\n    pil_img = Image.fromarray(img).convert(\\\"RGB\\\")\\n    pil_img = pil_img.resize((224, 224), Image.LANCZOS)\\n    return center_crop_image(pil_img, crop_scale=0.9)\\n\\n\\ndef quat2axisangle(quat):\\n    q = quat.copy()\\n    q[3] = np.clip(q[3], -1.0, 1.0)\\n    den = np.sqrt(1.0 - q[3] * q[3])\\n    if math.isclose(den, 0.0):\\n        return np.zeros(3)\\n    return (q[:3] * 2.0 * math.acos(q[3])) / den\\n\\n\\ndef get_proprio(obs, norm_stats):\\n    eef_pos = obs[\\\"robot0_eef_pos\\\"]\\n    eef_quat = obs[\\\"robot0_eef_quat\\\"]\\n    gripper_qpos = obs[\\\"robot0_gripper_qpos\\\"]\\n    axis_angle = quat2axisangle(eef_quat)\\n    proprio = np.concatenate([eef_pos, axis_angle, gripper_qpos]).astype(np.float64)\\n\\n    q01 = np.array(norm_stats[\\\"q01\\\"])\\n    q99 = np.array(norm_stats[\\\"q99\\\"])\\n    mask = np.array(norm_stats.get(\\\"mask\\\", [True] * len(proprio)))\\n    normalized = np.where(mask, 2.0 * (proprio - q01) / (q99 - q01 + 1e-8) - 1.0, proprio)\\n    return np.clip(normalized, -1.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Action generation\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef get_action_chunk(vla, processor, obs, task_label, action_head, proprio_proj):\\n    primary_img = preprocess_image(obs, \\\"agentview_image\\\")\\n    prompt = f\\\"In: What action should the robot take to {task_label.lower()}?\\\\nOut:\\\"\\n    inputs = processor(prompt, primary_img).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\\n\\n    if NUM_IMAGES_IN_INPUT > 1:\\n        wrist_img = preprocess_image(obs, \\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\")\\n        wrist_inputs = processor(prompt, wrist_img).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\\n        inputs[\\\"pixel_values\\\"] = torch.cat(\\n            [inputs[\\\"pixel_values\\\"], wrist_inputs[\\\"pixel_values\\\"]], dim=1\\n        )\\n\\n    proprio = None\\n    if USE_PROPRIO:\\n        proprio_norm_stats = vla.norm_stats[UNNORM_KEY][\\\"proprio\\\"]\\n        proprio = get_proprio(obs, proprio_norm_stats)\\n\\n    with torch.inference_mode():\\n        actions, _ = vla.predict_action(\\n            **inputs,\\n            unnorm_key=UNNORM_KEY,\\n            do_sample=False,\\n            proprio=proprio,\\n            proprio_projector=proprio_proj,\\n            action_head=action_head,\\n        )\\n\\n    return [actions[i] for i in range(len(actions))]\\n\\n\\ndef normalize_gripper_action(action, binarize=True):\\n    a = action.copy()\\n    a[..., -1] = 2.0 * a[..., -1] - 1.0\\n    if binarize:\\n        a[..., -1] = np.sign(a[..., -1])\\n    return a\\n\\n\\ndef invert_gripper_action(action):\\n    a = action.copy()\\n    a[..., -1] *= -1.0\\n    return a\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Main eval loop\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef main():\\n    args = parse_args()\\n    set_seed(args.seed)\\n    max_steps = SUITE_MAX_STEPS.get(args.suite, 280)\\n\\n    vla, processor, action_head, proprio_proj = load_model()\\n\\n    hook_handle = vla.projector.register_forward_hook(RandomPruningHook(args.k_ratio))\\n    print(f\\\"[*] Random pruning k={args.k_ratio} on vla.projector\\\", flush=True)\\n\\n    from libero.libero import benchmark, get_libero_path\\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\\n\\n    benchmark_dict = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()\\n    task_suite = benchmark_dict[args.suite]()\\n    n_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\\n    print(f\\\"[*] {args.suite}: {n_tasks} tasks x {args.num_episodes} eps = {n_tasks * args.num_episodes} total\\\",\\n          flush=True)\\n\\n    per_task_results = {}\\n    total_successes = 0\\n    total_episodes = 0\\n    t_start = time_mod.time()\\n\\n    for task_id in range(n_tasks):\\n        task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\\n        task_description = task.language\\n        task_bddl_file = os.path.join(\\n            get_libero_path(\\\"bddl_files\\\"), task.problem_folder, task.bddl_file\\n        )\\n        env = OffScreenRenderEnv(\\n            bddl_file_name=task_bddl_file, camera_heights=256, camera_widths=256\\n        )\\n        env.seed(0)\\n\\n        task_successes = 0\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n[Task {task_id+1}/{n_tasks}] {task_description}\\\", flush=True)\\n\\n        for ep in range(args.num_episodes):\\n            env.reset()\\n            init_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)\\n            obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[ep])\\n            done = False\\n            t = 0\\n            action_queue = deque(maxlen=NUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS)\\n\\n            while t < max_steps + args.num_steps_wait:\\n                try:\\n                    if t < args.num_steps_wait:\\n                        obs, reward, done, info = env.step([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1])\\n                        t += 1\\n                        continue\\n\\n                    if len(action_queue) == 0:\\n                        action_list = get_action_chunk(\\n                            vla, processor, obs, task_description, action_head, proprio_proj\\n                        )\\n                        for a in action_list:\\n                            action_queue.append(a)\\n\\n                    action = action_queue.popleft()\\n                    action = normalize_gripper_action(action, binarize=True)\\n                    action = invert_gripper_action(action)\\n                    obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\\n                    if done:\\n                        task_successes += 1\\n                        total_successes += 1\\n                        break\\n                    t += 1\\n                except Exception as e:\\n                    print(f\\\"  Exception t={t}: {e}\\\", flush=True)\\n                    import traceback; traceback.print_exc()\\n                    break\\n\\n            total_episodes += 1\\n            if (ep + 1) % 10 == 0:\\n                elapsed = time_mod.time() - t_start\\n                eps_per_min = total_episodes / (elapsed / 60) if elapsed > 0 else 0\\n                print(f\\\"  Ep {ep+1}/{args.num_episodes} task_sr={task_successes}/{ep+1} \\\"\\n                      f\\\"({eps_per_min:.1f} eps/min)\\\", flush=True)\\n\\n        task_sr = task_successes / args.num_episodes\\n        per_task_results[task_description] = {\\n            \\\"success_rate\\\": task_sr,\\n            \\\"successes\\\": task_successes,\\n            \\\"episodes\\\": args.num_episodes,\\n        }\\n        print(f\\\"  => Task SR: {task_sr:.3f} ({task_successes}/{args.num_episodes})\\\", flush=True)\\n        env.close()\\n\\n    overall_sr = total_successes / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0\\n    elapsed = time_mod.time() - t_start\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*60}\\\", flush=True)\\n    print(f\\\"Overall SR: {overall_sr:.4f} ({total_successes}/{total_episodes})\\\", flush=True)\\n    print(f\\\"Time: {elapsed/60:.1f} min\\\", flush=True)\\n\\n    results = {\\n        \\\"model\\\": \\\"openvla-7b-oft-libero-object\\\",\\n        \\\"mode\\\": \\\"random_prune\\\",\\n        \\\"suite\\\": args.suite,\\n        \\\"k_ratio\\\": args.k_ratio,\\n        \\\"overall_success_rate\\\": overall_sr,\\n        \\\"total_successes\\\": total_successes,\\n        \\\"total_episodes\\\": total_episodes,\\n        \\\"per_task\\\": per_task_results,\\n        \\\"num_open_loop_steps\\\": NUM_OPEN_LOOP_STEPS,\\n        \\\"num_images_in_input\\\": NUM_IMAGES_IN_INPUT,\\n        \\\"use_proprio\\\":
